*Qirun Zeng,Xuchuang Wang,Jiayi Shen,Xutong Liu,Fang Kong,Jinhang Zuo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文研究广义线性赌博机中固定置信度下的最优臂识别问题，提出了一种混合反馈模型和基于似然比的置信序列方法，设计了一个自适应的Track-and-Stop算法来提高样本效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究混合反馈模型下的最佳臂识别问题，其中学习者可以在每轮查询单臂的绝对奖励反馈或臂对的相对（对决）反馈。现有方法在处理这种异构的广义线性观测方面存在局限性，需要一种统一的方法来有效利用两种反馈类型。

Method: 1. 提出了基于似然比的置信序列，统一处理异构的广义线性观测，并在自协调假设下得到显式椭球形置信集。2. 基于该置信集设计了混合Track-and-Stop算法，通过在臂和臂对的联合动作空间上跟踪极小极大最优设计来自适应分配查询。3. 将框架扩展到成本感知设置，考虑跨反馈模态的异构获取成本。

Result: 建立了δ-正确性，并提供了停止时间的高概率上界。实证实验表明，所提出的算法相对于基线方法显著提高了样本效率。

Conclusion: 该论文提出了一种统一的置信序列框架，能够有效处理广义线性赌博机中的混合反馈（绝对奖励和相对对决反馈），并通过自适应算法显著提高了样本效率，同时支持成本感知的扩展。

Abstract: We study fixed-confidence best arm identification in generalized linear bandits under a hybrid feedback model: at each round, the learner may query either (i) absolute reward feedback from a single arm or (ii) relative (dueling) feedback from an arm pair, both governed by generalized linear models. We introduce a likelihood-ratio--based confidence sequence that unifies heterogeneous generalized linear observations and yields an explicit ellipsoidal confidence set under a self-concordance assumption. Building on this confidence set, we propose a hybrid Track-and-Stop algorithm that adaptively allocates queries by tracking a minimax-optimal design over a joint action space of arms and pairs. We establish $δ$-correctness and provide high-probability upper bounds on the stopping time. We further extend the framework to a cost-aware setting that accounts for heterogeneous acquisition costs across feedback modalities. Empirical experiments demonstrate that the proposed algorithms significantly improve sample efficiency over baseline methods.

</details>


### [179] [Confidence is the key: how conformal prediction enhances the generative design of permeable peptides](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05770)
*Laura van Weesep,Sunay Chankeshwara,Leonardo De Maria,Florian David,Ola Engkvist,Gökçe Geylan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了一种结合强化学习引导的生成模型与保形预测不确定性量化方法的新框架，用于设计具有膜渗透性的环状肽药物。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的基于强化学习的分子生成框架使用预测模型作为优化目标，但这些模型的适用域有限。当强化学习探索化学空间时，可能会产生超出预测模型适用域的分子，导致预测不可靠，将设计导向高奖励但高不确定性的化学空间。环状肽因其可修饰性和大相互作用表面而具有治疗潜力，但与小分子相比研究不足，特别是膜渗透性设计仍然具有挑战性。

Method: 开发了一个强化学习引导的生成框架，使用基于保形预测的不确定性感知渗透性预测器作为评分组件。保形预测作为一种不确定性量化方法，在校准模型的基础上根据用户定义的置信水平评估设计。该方法奖励生成具有保形预测信息预测的肽，同时阻止在预测器适用域外的探索。

Result: 该方法提高了肽优化过程的可靠性和效率，同时阻止了在预测器适用域外的探索。首次将生成建模与保形预测相结合，弥合了预测不确定性与强化学习引导探索之间的差距。

Conclusion: 该研究提出了一种创新的组合方法，将强化学习引导的生成建模与保形预测不确定性量化相结合，有效解决了药物设计中预测模型适用域有限的问题，为环状肽等复杂分子的设计提供了更可靠和高效的优化框架。

Abstract: Generative models coupled with reinforcement learning (RL), such as REINVENT and PepINVENT, have emerged as a powerful framework for de novo molecular design. During the ideation process these generative frameworks utilize various predictive models as part of the optimization objectives. However, the utility of the predictive models can be limited by their domain of applicability. When RL is used to explore the chemical space with predictive models, it can suggest molecules that lie outside the predictor's domain of applicability. As a result, the predictions may become less reliable, potentially steering designs into high reward but also high uncertainty chemical spaces. This is particularly pronounced for cyclic peptides which show therapeutic promise due to their modifiability and large interaction surfaces but are understudied compared to small molecules. While passive membrane permeation in cyclic peptides has attracted interest, identifying optimal permeable designs remains challenging yet crucial for targeting intracellular sites. We present an RL-guided generative framework that designs permeable cyclic peptides using an uncertainty-aware permeability predictor as the scoring component. To address predictive uncertainty, especially impacted by novel chemistry, we integrate conformal prediction (CP) as our uncertainty quantification method. CP assesses designs based on the calibrated model under a user-defined confidence level. We demonstrate that rewarding generated peptides with CP-informed predictions improves both reliability and efficiency of peptide optimization process. This also discourages exploration outside the predictor's applicability domain. This approach bridges the gap between predictive uncertainty and RL-guided exploration, showing how generative modelling and conformal prediction can be combined for the first time.

</details>


### [180] [CircuitFormer: A Circuit Language Model for Analog Topology Design from Natural Language Prompt](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05773)
*Md Touhidul Islam,Sujan Kumar Saha,Farimah Farahmandi,Mark Tehranipoor*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出了CKT（电路标记器）和CircuitFormer模型，通过新型电路图标记化方法和专有数据集，显著提升了模拟电路设计的自动化能力，在语法正确性和功能成功率上超越了现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模拟电路设计自动化是EDA领域的长期挑战。虽然基于Transformer的大语言模型在软件代码生成方面取得了革命性进展，但其应用于模拟硬件设计面临两个关键限制：1）缺乏包含自然语言描述和相应网表的模拟设计数据集；2）通用标记器（如BPE）无法有效捕获电路的固有图结构。

Method: 1. 构建了迄今为止最大的注释模拟电路网表数据集，包含31,341个网表-自然语言描述对；2. 提出CKT（电路标记器），一种新颖的电路图标记器，通过显式挖掘频繁子电路来编码网表连接性；3. 训练了CircuitFormer，一个511M参数的编码器-解码器Transformer模型。

Result: CKT在电路拓扑表示方面优于标准BPE，序列长度减少57%，压缩比提升2.3倍，使用固定512大小的词汇表。CircuitFormer在所有主要模拟电路类别中达到100%语法正确率和83%功能成功率，分别比最先进的开源LLM提升10%和14%，同时参数减少了240倍。

Conclusion: 该研究通过构建大规模数据集和开发专门的电路标记化方法，成功将LLM技术应用于模拟电路设计自动化。CKT的创新标记化策略和CircuitFormer的高性能表现，为EDA领域提供了有效的解决方案，推动了模拟电路设计自动化的进展。

Abstract: Automating analog circuit design remains a longstanding challenge in Electronic Design Automation (EDA). While Transformer-based Large Language Models (LLMs) have revolutionized software code generation, their application to analog hardware design is hindered by two critical limitations: (i) the scarcity of analog design datasets containing natural language description of a design and its corresponding netlist, and (ii) the inefficiency of general-purpose tokenizers (e.g., Byte Pair Encoding (BPE)) in capturing the inherent graph structure of circuits. To bridge this gap, first, we curate the largest annotated dataset of analog circuit netlists to date, comprising 31,341 netlist-natural language description pairs across all major circuit classes. Furthermore, we propose Circuit Tokenizer (CKT), a novel circuit graph tokenizer designed to encode netlist connectivity by explicitly mining frequent subcircuits. In terms of scalability, CKT overcomes the bottleneck of prior circuit graph serialization methods where vocabulary size scales linearly with maximum number of components in the dataset, n_max, (O(n_max)); instead, CKT decouples vocabulary growth from circuit complexity, achieving a constant O(1) complexity. Empirically, CKT outperforms standard BPE on circuit topology representation, reducing sequence length by 57% and achieving a 2.3x superior compression ratio using a compact, fixed vocabulary of size 512. Leveraging this optimized tokenization, we train a circuit-specific language model, CircuitFormer, a 511M parameter encoder-decoder transformer. Our model achieves 100% syntactic correctness and an 83% functional success rate across all major analog circuit categories, outperforming state-of-the-art open-source LLMs by 10% and 14%, respectively, while requiring 240x fewer parameters. The dataset is publicly available at https://huggingface.co/datasets/touhid314/cktformer-dataset.

</details>


### [181] [HEDP: A Hybrid Energy-Distance Prompt-based Framework for Domain Incremental Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05776)
*Yu Feng,Zhen Tian,Haoran Luo,Xie Yu,Diancheng Cheng,Haoyue Zheng,Shuai Lyu,Ping Zong,Lianyuan Li,Xin Ge,Yifan Zhu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了基于亥姆霍兹自由能的混合能量-距离提示（HEDP）框架，用于域增量学习，通过能量正则化损失和混合加权机制改善领域表示的可分离性，在CORe50等多个基准上实现了2.57%的准确率提升


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 域增量学习中，领域偏移常常导致严重的性能下降，需要模型在不重新训练的情况下持续适应新的数据域。现有方法在处理开放世界适应性方面存在局限性

Method: 提出混合能量-距离提示（HEDP）框架，包含：1）能量正则化损失来增强领域表示的可分离性；2）混合能量-距离加权机制，融合基于能量和基于距离的线索来改善领域选择和泛化能力

Result: 在多个基准测试（包括CORe50）上实验验证，HEDP在未见领域上实现了2.57%的准确率提升，有效缓解了灾难性遗忘并增强了开放世界适应性

Conclusion: HEDP框架为域增量学习提供了一个有效的解决方案，通过能量正则化和混合加权机制改善了领域表示和选择能力，在实际应用中具有良好前景

Abstract: Domain Incremental Learning is a critical scenario that requires models to continuously adapt to new data domains without retraining. However, domain shifts often cause severe performance degradation. To address this, we propose Hybrid Energy-Distance Prompt, a domain-incremental framework inspired by Helmholtz free energy. HEDP introduces an energy regularization loss to enhance the separability of domain representations and a hybrid energy-distance weighted mechanism that fuses energy-based and distance-based cues to improve domain selection and generalization. Experiments on multiple benchmarks, including CORe50, show that HEDP achieves superior performance on unseen domains with a 2.57\% accuracy gain, effectively mitigating catastrophic forgetting and enhancing open-world adaptability. Our code is \href{https://github.com/dannis97500/HEDP/}{available here}.

</details>


### [182] [AGPO: Asymmetric Group Policy Optimization for Verifiable Reasoning and Search Ads Relevance at JD](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05826)
*Yang Xu,Kun Yao,Yiming Deng,Zheng Fang,Kai Ming Ting,Ming Pang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出AGPO方法，通过不对称分组策略优化来解决强化学习可验证奖励中的推理边界缩小问题，在数学基准测试和工业应用中均取得显著性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前基于可验证奖励的强化学习（RLVR）方法虽然提高了采样效率，但并未引出新的推理模式，反而导致训练模型的推理能力边界相比基础模型缩小。基础模型在大样本量下反而能达到更高的覆盖率。

Method: 提出非对称分组策略优化（AGPO）方法：1）采用负主导强化策略来抑制错误推理路径，保持基础模型的探索能力；2）采用分组优势机制进行正向强化，根据组内方差缩放正向更新，使模型能够关注到罕见正确路径，同时抑制平凡路径的更新。

Result: 在五个数学基准测试中达到最先进准确率，并一致提升大规模下的pass@k性能。在搜索引擎广告相关性优化的大规模工业应用中，AGPO有效提升了数据标注质量，使下游学生模型获得显著的性能增益。

Conclusion: AGPO方法通过非对称的正负强化策略有效解决了RLVR方法中的推理边界缩小问题，既保持了探索能力又优化了正确路径的强化，在理论和应用层面都表现出优越性能。

Abstract: Reinforcement Learning with Verifiable Rewards (RLVR) has demonstrated notable success in enhancing the reasoning performance of large language models (LLMs). However, recent studies reveal that while current RLVR methods improve sampling efficiency towards correct paths, they do not elicit fundamentally new reasoning patterns. Instead, the reasoning capability boundary of trained models often narrows compared to their base models, with base models achieving higher coverage at large sample sizes. In this work, we propose Asymmetric Group Policy Optimization (AGPO) to counteract this boundary shrinkage. AGPO adopts a negative-dominant reinforcement strategy to suppress incorrect reasoning paths, maintaining the base model's exploration capacity. For positive reinforcement, AGPO adopts a group advantage mechanism, which scales positive updates based on intra-group variance, allowing the model to focus on rare correct paths while suppressing updates from trivial paths. Our experiments on five mathematical benchmarks demonstrate that AGPO achieves state-of-the-art accuracy while consistently improving pass@$k$ performance at scale. In a large-scale industrial application for search ads relevance optimization, AGPO effectively enhances the quality of the data annotation, leading to substantial performance gains in downstream student models.

</details>


### [183] [On the Role of Language Representations in Auto-Bidding: Findings and Implications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05833)
*Guanyu Zhu,Jining Luan,Hanwen Du,Xinyu Fang,Sibo Xu,Ersheng Ni,Hongji Li,Jincheng Fang,Ronghao Chen,Huacan Wang,Xuanqi Lan,Yongxin Ni,Yiqi Sun,Youhua Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SemBid是一个新颖的自动竞价框架，通过在大语言模型编码的语义嵌入和数值轨迹之间进行token级集成，提升了实时广告市场中自动竞价策略的泛化能力和可控性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前自动竞价方法依赖紧凑的数值状态表示，虽然能隐式捕捉交付动态，但对表示和控制高层意图、演化反馈和操作式战略指导的支持有限。同时，尽管大语言模型能编码丰富的语义信息，但如何有效整合而不牺牲数值精度仍不清楚。

Method: 提出SemBid框架，将LLM编码的语义信息注入离线竞价轨迹的token级别。引入三种语义输入：任务（Task）、历史（History）和策略（Strategy）。将这些语义作为token与数值轨迹token一起输入，利用自注意力机制进行集成。

Result: 在多样化场景和预算机制下，SemBid超越了离线强化学习和生成序列建模的基准方法，在整体性能、约束满足度和鲁棒性方面表现更一致。

Conclusion: LLM嵌入包含竞价相关线索但不能完全替代数值特征；只有通过仔细的语义-数值集成而非简单拼接才能带来收益。SemBid通过token级语义注入有效提升了自动竞价策略的泛化能力和可控性。

Abstract: Auto-bidding is a crucial task in real-time advertising markets, where policies must optimize long-horizon value under delivery constraints (e.g., budget and CPA). Existing methods for auto-bidding rely on compact numerical state representations: while they can implicitly capture delivery dynamics, they offer limited support for explicitly representing and controlling high-level intent, evolving feedback, and operator-style strategic guidance in real campaigns. Meanwhile, Large Language Models (LLMs) offer a powerful method for encoding semantic information, it remains unclear when LLMs help and how to integrate them without sacrificing numerical precision. Through systematic preliminary studies, we find that (1) LLM embeddings contain bidding-relevant cues yet cannot replace numerical features, and (2) gains emerge only with careful semantic--numeric integration rather than naive concatenation. Motivated by these findings, we propose \textit{SemBid}, a novel auto-bidding framework that injects LLM-encoded semantics into offline bidding trajectories at the token level. SemBid introduces three semantic inputs: \textit{Task}, \textit{History}, and \textit{Strategy}. It injects these semantics as tokens alongside numerical trajectory tokens and uses self-attention to integrate them, improving controllability and generalization across objectives. Across diverse scenarios and budget regimes, SemBid outperforms competitive baselines from offline RL and generative sequence modeling, with more consistent gains in overall performance, constraint satisfaction, and robustness. Our code is available at: \href{https://github.com/AlanYu04/SemBid-KDD2026}{\textcolor{blue}{here}}.

</details>


### [184] [Taklif.AI: LLM-Powered Platform for Interest-Based Personalized College Assignments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05842)
*Zaki Kurdya,Mohammed Zuqlam,Salem Amassi,Shady Telbany,Motaz Saad*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Taklif.AI是一个利用大语言模型自动生成个性化作业的平台，通过整合学生的课外兴趣和文化背景，解决了传统作业"一刀切"导致的参与度低和抄袭问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 教育工作者在创建吸引人、个性化的作业方面面临重大挑战。传统的"一刀切"作业经常导致学生参与度下降，并增加了抄袭等不道德行为的依赖。现有AI教育平台主要基于学业成绩进行个性化，而忽视了学生的课外兴趣和文化背景。

Method: Taklif.AI采用基于AWS的serverless架构，使用Next.js前端，以Llama 3.3 70B作为主要大语言模型，通过LiteLLM进行多提供商负载均衡，使用LangChain进行提示编排。系统包含结构化的提示工程管道，具有输入和输出的保障机制，能够整合学生的课外兴趣和文化背景。

Result: 初步用户接受度测试有68名参与者（65名学生和3名教育工作者），84%的参与者认为个性化功能有益，表明平台获得了积极反响。平台展示了当前的能力和局限性。

Conclusion: Taklif.AI展示了通过整合学生课外兴趣和文化背景来生成个性化作业的可行性。平台在用户接受度方面获得积极反馈，但需要进一步进行严谨的实证研究来评估学习效果，并明确了未来研究方向。

Abstract: Educators face significant challenges in creating engaging, personalized assignments that accommodate students' diverse interests and cognitive abilities. Traditional one-size-fits-all assignments frequently lead to decreased student engagement and increased reliance on unethical practices such as plagiarism. To address these challenges, we present Taklif.AI, a platform that leverages Large Language Models (LLMs) to automatically generate personalized assignments tailored to individual student interests. Unlike existing AI-powered educational platforms that personalize based on academic performance metrics alone, Taklif.AI incorporates students' extracurricular interests and cultural contexts into the assignment generation process through a structured prompt engineering pipeline with input and output guardrails. The platform employs a serverless architecture on AWS with Next.js, using Llama 3.3 70B as the primary LLM via LiteLLM for multi-provider load balancing and LangChain for prompt orchestration. We describe the system architecture, the prompt design methodology, and the guardrails framework that ensures output quality. Preliminary user acceptance testing with 68 participants (65 students and 3 educators) indicates positive reception, with 84% of participants rating the personalization feature as beneficial. We discuss the platform's current capabilities and limitations, and outline directions for rigorous empirical evaluation of learning outcomes.

</details>


### [185] [XDecomposer: Learning Prior-Free Set Decomposition for Multiphase X-ray Diffraction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05866)
*Hanyu Gao,Bin Cao,Yunyue Su,Tong-Yi Zhang,Qiang Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: XDecomposer是一个无先验框架，用于多相X射线衍射图案的联合分解和识别，无需候选相列表、结构模板或相数先验知识，通过集合预测问题解决了多相衍射分析的瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多相粉末X射线衍射分析是结构识别的关键瓶颈，因为实际合成常产生复杂混合物，现有方法大多假设单相输入，在多相设置中失效，需要无先验的多相分析方法。

Method: 将多相衍射分析构建为集合预测问题，模型推断无序的相分辨组分集合、混合物比例和相应的结构表示；采用相查询驱动分解机制和衍射一致的物理重建，实现准确的源分离同时保持晶体学保真度。

Result: 在模拟和实验数据集上的广泛实验表明，XDecomposer显著提高了重建精度和相识别准确性，同时保持对未见混合物的强泛化能力。

Conclusion: 该方法为数据驱动的源分辨多相XRD分析提供了实用途径，减少了对先验指导的迭代相匹配的长期依赖，推动了多相粉末XRD分析的进展。

Abstract: Multiphase powder X-ray diffraction (PXRD) analysis remains a fundamental bottleneck in structure identification, as real-world synthesis often produces complex mixtures whose constituent phases (components) cannot be reliably disentangled. While recent advances in representation-based crystal retrieval and generation suggest the possibility of inferring structures directly from PXRD, existing approaches largely assume single-phase inputs and break down in multiphase settings. Here, we present XDecomposer, a prior-free framework for joint decomposition and identification of multiphase XRD patterns without requiring candidate phase lists, structural templates, or prior knowledge of phase number. We formulate multiphase diffraction analysis as a set prediction problem, where the model infers an unordered set of phase-resolved components, their mixture proportions, and corresponding structural representations within a unified architecture. A phase-query-driven decomposition mechanism, together with diffraction-consistent physical reconstruction, enables accurate source separation while preserving crystallographic fidelity. Extensive experiments on both simulated and experimental datasets show that XDecomposer substantially improves reconstruction accuracy and phase identification across diverse chemical systems, while maintaining strong generalization to unseen mixtures. These results provide a practical route toward data-driven, source-resolved multiphase XRD analysis and reduce long-standing dependence on prior-guided iteratively phase matching. The code is openly available at https://github.com/Licht0812/XDecomposer

</details>


### [186] [Null Space Constrained Contrastive Visual Forgetting for MLLM Unlearning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05909)
*Yuhang Wang,Zhenxing Niu,Haoxuan Ji,Guangyu He,Linlin Zhang,Haichang Gao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出一种多模态大语言模型(MLLM)的遗忘学习方法，专注于遗忘目标视觉知识同时保留非目标视觉知识和所有文本知识，通过对比视觉遗忘机制和零空间约束实现高效遗忘与知识保留的平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器遗忘的核心挑战是在目标知识移除与非目标知识保留之间取得平衡。在多模态大语言模型(MLLMs)中，这一挑战更为突出，因为知识进一步分为紧密交织的视觉和文本模态。现有方法难以在遗忘目标视觉知识的同时，充分保留非目标视觉知识和文本知识。

Method: 1. 冻结LLM主干，仅微调视觉模块实现遗忘；2. 提出对比视觉遗忘(CVF)机制，将目标视觉知识与保留的视觉知识分离，引导目标视觉概念表征到特征空间的适当区域；3. 识别与保留知识相关的零空间，将遗忘过程约束在该空间内，显著减轻知识保留退化；4. 扩展方法到连续遗忘场景，处理顺序到达的遗忘请求。

Result: 在多样化基准测试上的广泛实验表明，该方法在有效遗忘和稳健知识保留之间实现了强大平衡。相比基线方法，在目标知识遗忘准确率、保留知识保持率和整体模型性能方面均有显著提升。

Conclusion: 提出的MLLM遗忘学习框架通过对比视觉遗忘机制和零空间约束，有效解决了多模态环境下的机器遗忘挑战。该方法不仅能处理静态遗忘场景，还能扩展到连续遗忘，为实际应用中的模型更新和安全隐私保护提供了可行解决方案。

Abstract: The core challenge of machine unlearning is to strike a balance between target knowledge removal and non-target knowledge retention. In the context of Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs), this challenge becomes even more pronounced, as knowledge is further divided into visual and textual modalities that are tightly intertwined. In this paper, we introduce an MLLM unlearning approach that aims to forget target visual knowledge while preserving non-target visual knowledge and all textual knowledge. Specifically, we freeze the LLM backbone and achieve unlearning by fine-tuning the visual module. First, we propose a Contrastive Visual Forgetting (CVF) mechanism to separate target visual knowledge from retained visual knowledge, guiding the representations of target visual concepts toward appropriate regions in the feature space. Second, we identify the null space associated with retained knowledge and constrain the unlearning process within this space, thereby significantly mitigating degradation in knowledge retention. Third, beyond static unlearning scenarios, we extend our approach to continual unlearning, where forgetting requests arrive sequentially. Extensive experiments across diverse benchmarks demonstrate that our approach achieves a strong balance between effective forgetting and robust knowledge retention.

</details>


### [187] [PREFER: Personalized Review Summarization with Online Preference Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05911)
*Millend Roy,Agostino Capponi,Vineet Goyal*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出一种基于在线学习的产品评论个性化摘要生成框架，通过迭代学习用户潜在偏好来生成定制化摘要


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前电商摘要系统生成通用静态摘要，无法适应不同用户对产品特性的关注差异，且用户偏好可能随时间动态变化，现有方法难以处理未知潜在偏好问题

Method: 采用在线学习框架，通过从生成的摘要中直接获取反馈来迭代优化对用户偏好的理解，为每个用户生成个性化摘要

Result: 在Amazon Reviews'23数据集上的控制模拟实验表明，在线偏好学习能更好对齐目标用户兴趣，同时保持摘要质量

Conclusion: 通过在线学习动态适应未知用户偏好的方法有效解决了电商评论个性化摘要问题，为生成更符合用户需求的摘要提供了新思路

Abstract: Product reviews significantly influence purchasing decisions on e-commerce platforms. However, the sheer volume of reviews can overwhelm users, obscuring the information most relevant to their specific needs. Current e-commerce summarization systems typically produce generic, static summaries that fail to account for the fact that (i) different users care about different product characteristics, and (ii) these preferences may evolve with interactions. To address the challenge of unknown latent preferences, we propose an online learning framework that generates personalized summaries for each user. Our system iteratively refines its understanding of user preferences by incorporating feedback directly from the generated summaries over time. We provide a case study using the Amazon Reviews'23 dataset, showing in controlled simulations that online preference learning improves alignment with target user interests while maintaining summary quality.

</details>


### [188] [Wisteria: A Unified Multi-Scale Feature Learning Framework for DNA Language Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05913)
*Weihua Wang,Haoji Li,Feilong Bao,Lei Yang,Guanglai Gao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Wisteria是一个多尺度基因组语言模型，通过结合门控扩张卷积和门控多层感知器来统一局部基序和全局依赖建模，并在多个实验设置中表现出色。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的DNA语言模型强调长距离token交互，但往往忽视了局部基序与全局依赖之间的相互作用。为了更全面地理解基因组的调控语法和语义，需要一种能够整合多尺度特征学习的统一框架。

Method: Wisteria基于Mamba架构，集成了三个关键技术：1) 门控扩张卷积用于捕获局部基序和调控模式；2) 门控多层感知器用于精炼全局依赖关系；3) 基于傅里叶的注意力机制，支持频域建模、周期扩展和长度泛化。

Result: 在包含短距离和长距离依赖的四种实验设置中，Wisteria在下游基准测试中相对于竞争性DNA语言模型基线表现出强大的性能。这表明模型能有效统一局部和全局依赖建模。

Conclusion: Wisteria成功地开发了一个统一的多尺度基因组语言模型框架，能够同时处理局部基序和全局依赖关系，为多尺度基因组序列分析提供了有效的解决方案，并在多个基准测试中验证了其优越性。

Abstract: DNA language model aims to decipher the regulatory grammar and semantic of genomes by capturing long range dependencies in DNA sequences. Existing methods emphasize long range token interactions but often ignore the interplay between local motifs and global dependencies. In this paper, we propose Wisteria, a genomic language model that integrates multi scale feature learning within a unified framework for DNA sequence. Specifically, Wisteria augments the Mamba based architecture with gated dilated convolutions to capture local motifs and regulatory patterns, while gated multilayer perceptrons refine global dependencies. We further introduce a Fourier based attention mechanism to support frequency domain modeling, periodic extension and length generalization. Across four experimental settings with both short and long range dependencies, Wisteria demonstrates strong performance on downstream benchmarks against competitive DNA language model baselines. These results indicate that Wisteria effectively unifies local and global dependency modeling for multi scale genomic sequence analysis.

</details>


### [189] [PRAISE: Prefix-Based Rollout Reuse in Agentic Search Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.03675)
*Erhan Zhang,Yiqun Chen,Zechun Niu,Wei Yang,Xiaochi Wei,Yan Gao,Yi Wu,Yao Hu,Jiaxin Mao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: PRAISE框架通过重用搜索轨迹前缀和引入中间步骤奖励，解决了智能搜索中数据利用率低和奖励稀疏的问题，提高了多跳问答任务的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前基于搜索的强化学习方法存在两个核心限制：训练时昂贵的长周期rollout未被充分利用，以及监督信号通常只在最终答案处可用，导致严重的奖励稀疏问题。

Method: PRAISE从完整的搜索轨迹中提取不同搜索轮次的前缀状态，从中引出中间答案，并使用这些前缀既构建额外的训练轨迹，又通过前缀间性能差异推导步骤级奖励。该方法使用单一共享模型同时进行搜索策略学习和前缀答案评估。

Result: 在多跳问答基准测试上的实验表明，PRAISE始终优于强基线方法。

Conclusion: PRAISE框架通过重用前缀轨迹和引入中间奖励，有效提升了智能搜索训练的数据效率和信用分配，无需额外人工标注或独立奖励模型，在多跳问答任务中表现出优越性能。

Abstract: In agentic search, large language models (LLMs) are trained to perform multi-turn retrieval and reasoning for complex tasks such as multi-hop question answering (QA). However, current search-based Reinforcement Learning (RL) methods suffer from two core limitations: expensive long-horizon rollouts are under-utilized during training, and supervision is typically available only at the final answer, resulting in severe reward sparsity. We present Prefix-based Rollout reuse for Agentic search with Intermediate Step rEwards (PRAISE), a framework for improving both data efficiency and credit assignment in agentic search training. Given a complete search trajectory, PRAISE extracts prefix states at different search turns, elicits intermediate answers from them, and uses these prefixes both to construct additional training trajectories and to derive step-level rewards from performance differences across prefixes. Our method uses a single shared model for both search policy learning and prefix answer evaluation, enabling joint optimization without extra human annotations or a separate reward model. Experiments on multi-hop QA benchmarks show that PRAISE consistently improves performance over strong baselines.

</details>


### [190] [Which Are the Low-Resource Languages of the Semantic Web?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05929)
*Ndeye-Emilie Mbengue,Pierre Monnin,Miguel Couceiro,Fabien Gandon*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出一种分析链接开放数据知识图谱中语言分布的方法，并基于DBpedia、BabelNet和Wikidata建立了多级语言分类体系，旨在为跨语言迁移选择合适的候选语言。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 新兴数字技术加剧了开放访问数据在高资源和低资源语言之间的现有鸿沟，许多社区被排除在全球数字化转型之外。多语言链接开放数据知识图谱可通过跨语言迁移缓解这一问题，但在该背景下缺乏明确量化的低资源语言定义。

Method: 提出一种分析方法来研究链接开放数据知识图谱中的语言分布，基于DBpedia、BabelNet和Wikidata构建初步的多级分类体系。该方法用于制定正式的低、中、高资源语言定义。

Result: 建立了基于三个主要知识图谱（DBpedia、BabelNet、Wikidata）的语言分布分析和多级分类框架，为跨语言迁移候选选择提供了量化基础。

Conclusion: 该研究为链接开放数据知识图谱领域提供了量化语言资源水平的框架，有助于通过跨语言迁移策略缓解数字鸿沟，未来可应用于选择适合的跨语言迁移候选语言。

Abstract: Emerging digital technologies are exacerbating the existing divide in Open Access Data (OAD) between high-and low-resource languages, excluding many communities from the global digital transformation. Multilingual Linked Open Data Knowledge Graphs (LOD KGs) could contribute to mitigating this divide through cross-lingual transfer; however, no clear quantitative definition of low-resource languages has yet been established in the context of LOD KGs. In this poster, we present a methodology to analyze the distribution of languages across LOD KGs and propose a preliminary multi-level categorization based on DBpedia, BabelNet, and Wikidata. This categorization is leveraged to bring a formal definition of low-, high-, and medium-resource languages that could be later leveraged to select cross-lingual transfer candidates.

</details>


### [191] [In Data or Invisible: Toward a Better Digital Representation of Low-Resource Languages with Knowledge Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05931)
*Ndeye-Emilie Mbengue*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该博士提案研究关联开放数据知识图谱中的语言覆盖度问题，通过分析DBpedia、BabelNet和Wikidata中语言的分布特征，并探索跨语言迁移和类比推理策略来提高低资源语言的覆盖率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 新兴数字技术正在加剧高资源和低资源语言之间在开放获取数据方面的鸿沟，许多语言社群被排除在全球数字化转型之外。关联开放数据知识图谱中存在显著的语言覆盖不平衡问题。

Method: 首先识别LOD中语言分布的关键变量（如维基百科文章数量和语言标记实体数），在三个主要多语言LOD KG（DBpedia、BabelNet、Wikidata）中进行分析。然后研究跨语言迁移候选选择对多语言KG补全的影响，探索基于语言接近度和标注对齐的策略，并研究利用类比推理（依赖相似性/差异性）来识别跨语言对应关系的方法。

Result: 通过分析揭示了LOD中语言的表征和分布情况。研究将探索不同策略对KG补全性能的影响，旨在提高低资源语言的覆盖度，但目前处于提案阶段，具体实验结果尚未获得。

Conclusion: 该研究旨在通过分析语言分布特征和开发跨语言知识图谱补全方法来缩小数字语言鸿沟，特别是通过语言接近度分析和类比推理策略来增强低资源语言在关联开放数据中的覆盖度。

Abstract: Emerging digital technologies are exacerbating the existing divide in Open Access Data (OAD) between high-and low-resource languages, excluding many communities from participating in the global digital transformation. In this PhD proposal, we aim to address this gap, focusing on the language coverage of Linked Open Data knowledge graphs (LOD KGs). First, we identify key variables that characterize language distribution in LOD, including the number of Wikipedia articles per language edition and the number of language-tagged entities in LOD KGs. These variables are analyzed across three major multilingual LOD KGs, DBpedia, BabelNet, and Wikidata, providing insights into the representation and distribution of languages within LOD. Building on this analysis, we intend to study the impact of cross-lingual transfer candidate selection on the task of multilingual KG completion. In particular, we plan to investigate strategies based on linguistic proximity and the availability of curated annotated alignments between languages. Language proximity also motivates us to explore the benefits of analogical reasoning that relies on (dis)similarities and has not yet been investigated to identify correspondences across languages to improve KG completion performance and enhance language coverage in LOD.

</details>


### [192] [ICU-Bench:Benchmarking Continual Unlearning in Multimodal Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05938)
*Yuhang Wang,Wenjie Mei,Junkai Zhang,Guangyu He,Zhenxing Niu,Haichang Gao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: ICU-Bench：一个持续多模态遗忘基准，专注于隐私敏感文档数据，包含医疗报告和劳动合同两个领域的1000个隐私档案、9500张图片、16000个问答对和100个遗忘任务，用于评估持续隐私删除场景下的多模态大语言模型遗忘能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态大语言模型在大规模多模态数据集上的训练引发严重的隐私担忧，需要有效的机器遗忘技术。然而现有基准主要关注静态或短序列场景，对现实部署中持续隐私删除请求的评估支持有限，这一空白需要填补。

Method: 构建ICU-Bench基准，包含来自医疗报告和劳动合同两个文档领域的1000个隐私敏感档案，总计9500张图像、16000个问答对和100个遗忘任务。引入新的持续遗忘指标，评估遗忘效果、历史遗忘保留、保留效用和整个持续遗忘过程的稳定性。

Result: 通过在ICU-Bench上进行代表性遗忘方法的广泛实验，发现现有方法在持续设置中普遍存在困难，在平衡遗忘质量、效用保留和长任务序列可扩展性方面显示出明显局限性。

Conclusion: 研究结果强调了需要专门为持续隐私删除设计的多模态遗忘方法。ICU-Bench基准填补了现有评估体系的空白，为开发更好的持续多模态遗忘技术提供了必要的基础设施。

Abstract: Although Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) have achieved remarkable progress across many domains, their training on large-scale multimodal datasets raises serious privacy concerns, making effective machine unlearning increasingly necessary. However, existing benchmarks mainly focus on static or short-sequence settings, offering limited support for evaluating continual privacy deletion requests in realistic deployments. To bridge this gap, we introduce ICU-Bench, a continual multimodal unlearning benchmark built on privacy-critical document data. ICU-Bench contains 1,000 privacy-sensitive profiles from two document domains, medical reports and labor contracts, with 9,500 images, 16,000 question-answer pairs, and 100 forget tasks. Additionally, new continual unlearning metrics are introduced, facilitating a comprehensive analysis of forgetting effectiveness, historical forgetting preservation, retained utility, and stability throughout the continual unlearning process. Through extensive experiments with representative unlearning methods on ICU-Bench, we show that existing methods generally struggle in continual settings and exhibit clear limitations in balancing forgetting quality, utility preservation, and scalability over long task sequences. These findings highlight the need for multimodal unlearning methods explicitly designed for continual privacy deletion.

</details>


### [193] [MAS-Algorithm: A Workflow for Solving Algorithmic Programming Problems with a Multi-Agent System](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05949)
*Yuliang Xu,Xiang Xu,Yao Wan,Hu Wei,Tong Jia*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: MAS-Algorithm：一个受竞技程序员和算法工程师实践启发的系统性多智能体工作流，用于算法问题求解，通过模块化阶段分解推理过程，在Qwen系列模型上实现平均6.48%的接受率提升


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 算法问题求解是评估AI编码系统结构化推理能力的严谨测试平台。现有方法主要依赖模型中心策略（架构修改和数据扩展），这些方法成本高且可解释性有限。其他利用外部工具或提示技术的方法（如思维链）往往碎片化且缺乏统一框架。

Method: 提出了MAS-Algorithm，一个受竞技程序员和算法工程师实践启发的系统性多智能体工作流。该框架将端到端求解过程分解为模块化阶段，支持结构化推理、工具集成和智能体间的灵活协调，设计强调严谨性和可扩展性，能泛化到多种问题类型。

Result: 在自构建的基准测试中，在多个Qwen系列模型上实现一致的改进，平均接受率提升6.48%。相比之下，相同数据上的参数高效微调仅带来0.89%的边际提升。在LiveCodeBench-Pro上进一步获得4.72%的提升，在其他准确性和效率指标上也有一致改进。通过定制替换和消融研究表明，单个智能体可贡献高达27.7%的改进。

Conclusion: MAS-Algorithm在推动AI驱动的算法推理方面显示出强大潜力，通过系统性的多智能体工作流有效克服现有方法的局限性，实现了显著的性能提升和更好的可解释性。

Abstract: Algorithmic problem solving serves as a rigorous testbed for evaluating structured reasoning in AI coding systems, as it directly reflects a model's ability to perform structured reasoning in complex scenarios.Existing approaches predominantly rely on model-centric strategies, such as architectural modifications and data scaling, which are costly and offer limited interpretability. Alternative methods leveraging external tools or prompting techniques (e.g., chain-of-thought) are often fragmented and lack a unified framework. In this paper, we propose MAS-Algorithm, a systematic multi-agent workflow for algorithmic problem solving inspired by the practices of competitive programmers and algorithm engineers. Our framework decomposes the end-to-end solving process into modular stages, enabling structured reasoning, tool integration, and flexible coordination among agents. The design emphasizes both rigor and extensibility, allowing it to generalize across diverse problem types.Experimental results on a self-constructed benchmark demonstrate consistent improvements across multiple Qwen series models, achieving an average gain of 6.48% in acceptance rate. In contrast, parameter-efficient fine-tuning on the same data yields only a marginal improvement of 0.89%. We further observe a 4.72% gain on LiveCodeBench-Pro, along with consistent improvements across additional accuracy and efficiency metrics.Beyond performance gains, we conduct comprehensive analyses to better understand the reasoning process within the workflow, including error patterns and cross-scenario behaviors. We further perform customized replacement and ablation studies to explore the upper bound of the framework, showing that individual agents can contribute improvements of up to 27.7%. These results highlight the strong potential of MAS-Algorithm for advancing AI-driven algorithmic reasoning.

</details>


### [194] [HaM-World: Soft-Hamiltonian World Models with Selective Memory for Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05951)
*Haoyun Tang,Haodong Cui,Keyao Xu,Kun Wang,Zhandong Mei*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: HaM-World提出了一种结构化世界模型，通过哈密顿力学分解潜在状态为(q,p)构型/动量子空间和上下文子空间c，使用Mamba状态空间记忆作为历史条件输入，实现了稳定的长时程规划和分布外泛化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有世界模型在规划时域增长或动态分布偏移时，想象的rollout会变得不稳定。作者认为这反映了规划器面向的潜在空间缺少两个结构：用于近似马尔可夫完备性的历史条件记忆，以及分离构型、动量和任务语义的几何组织。

Method: HaM-World将潜在状态分解为规范(q,p)子空间（构型和动量）和上下文子空间c。使用Mamba选择性状态空间记忆作为历史条件输入到相同的潜在动态中。(q,p)通过能量导出的哈密顿向量场加上可学习的残差/控制动态演化，而c捕获语义、耗散和非保守因素。

Result: 在四个DeepMind Control Suite任务上：达到最高平均AUC（117.9，+9.5%），将长时程rollout误差降低到强基线模型的45%，在{3,5,7}个MSE单元中赢得11/12。在12种分布外扰动下，HaM-World在每个条件下都获得了最高回报，平均分布外回报增益在Finger Spin上为10.2%，在Reacher Easy上为13.6%。

Conclusion: HaM-World通过结构化潜在状态表示和哈密顿动态设计，提供了稳定的模型基规划框架。机制诊断显示有界的无动作哈密顿能量漂移、策略rollout下的结构化能量变化以及相干控制诱导的能量转移，支持了预期的软哈密顿动态设计。

Abstract: World models enable model-based planning through learned latent dynamics, but imagined rollouts become unstable as the planning horizon grows or the dynamics distribution shifts. We argue that this instability reflects two missing structures in planner-facing latents: history-conditioned memory for approximate Markov completeness, and geometric organization that separates configuration, momentum, and task semantics. We propose HaM-World (HMW), a structured world model that decomposes the latent state into a canonical (q, p) subspace and a context subspace c, while using Mamba selective state-space memory as the history-conditioned input to the same latent dynamics. Within this interface, (q, p) evolves through an energy-derived Hamiltonian vector field plus learnable residual/control dynamics, while c captures semantic, dissipative, and non-conservative factors. This gives the planner a single latent state shared by dynamics prediction, reward/value estimation, imagined rollouts, and CEM action search. On four DeepMind Control Suite tasks, HaM-World reaches the highest Avg. AUC (117.9, +9.5%), reduces long-horizon rollout error to 45% of a strong baseline model, and wins 11/12 k in {3,5,7} MSE cells. Under 12 OOD perturbations spanning dynamics shifts, action delay, and observation masking, HaM-World achieves the highest return in every condition, with average OOD-return gains of 10.2% on Finger Spin and 13.6% on Reacher Easy. Mechanism diagnostics further show bounded action-free Hamiltonian-energy drift, structured energy variation under policy rollouts, and coherent control-induced energy transfer, supporting the intended Soft-Hamiltonian dynamics design.

</details>


### [195] [Temporal Smoothness Doubly Robust Learning for Debiased Knowledge Tracing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05958)
*Peilin Zhan,Wei Chen,Weilin Chen,Shuyi Pan,Ruichu Cai*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了时序平滑双鲁棒(TSDR)框架，用于解决知识追踪中的选择偏差问题，通过在双鲁棒估计中引入时序平滑正则化来降低方差，提升训练稳定性与性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 知识追踪依赖选择性观测的教育日志数据，练习推荐和学生选择的非随机性导致严重选择偏差。现有方法忽视此问题，使用标准经验风险训练会产生有偏掌握度估计，误差在后续推荐中累积。

Method: 提出时序平滑双鲁棒(TSDR)框架：1) 基于双鲁棒估计理论，结合倾向性模型和误差插补模型；2) 推导出明确刻画估计器方差影响的泛化界，识别时序平滑性是控制方差的关键因素；3) 引入平滑正则化器联合优化知识追踪预测器和插补模型，减少方差同时保持双鲁棒的无偏性保证。

Result: 在多个真实世界基准测试上的实验表明，TSDR能持续增强各种最先进的知识追踪骨干模型，证明了基于原则的偏差校正在知识追踪中的重要作用。

Conclusion: 本文解决了知识追踪中的选择偏差问题，通过理论分析揭示了估计器方差对序列决策的影响机制，提出的TSDR框架在保持无偏性同时有效控制方差，为基于原则的偏差校正提供了理论支持和实用解决方案。

Abstract: Knowledge Tracing (KT) is fundamental to intelligent education systems, yet relies on educational logs that are selectively observed. The non-random nature of exercise recommendations and student choices inevitably induces severe selection bias. Most existing KT methods neglect this issue, training on observed logs using standard empirical risk, which yields biased mastery estimates and accumulates errors in subsequent recommendations. To address this, we introduce a doubly robust (DR) formulation for KT that integrates a propensity model with an error imputation model, theoretically guaranteeing unbiasedness if either model is accurate. Beyond unbiasedness, in the sequential setting of KT, we identify that the estimator's performance is compromised by variance-dependent stochastic deviations that accumulate over time, thereby causing training instability and limiting performance. To mitigate this, we derive a generalization bound that explicitly characterizes the impact of estimator variance and identifies temporal smoothness as a key factor in controlling it. Building on these theoretical insights, we propose the Temporal Smoothness Doubly Robust (TSDR) framework. TSDR jointly optimizes the KT predictor and the imputation model with a smoothness regularizer, effectively reducing variance while preserving the unbiasedness guarantee of DR. Experiments on multiple real-world benchmarks demonstrate that TSDR consistently enhances various state-of-the-art KT backbones, underscoring the vital role of principled bias correction in KT.

</details>


### [196] [From Coordinate Matching to Structural Alignment: Rethinking Prototype Alignment in Heterogeneous Federated Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05959)
*Xinghao Wu,Jianwei Niu,Guogang Zhu,Xuefeng Liu,Shaojie Tang,Jiayuan Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: FedSAF提出了一种从坐标对齐转向结构对齐的异构联邦学习方法，通过对齐类间语义关系而非强制共享特征子空间，在保持模型异质性的同时提升分类性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有异构联邦学习中的原型对齐方法沿用同质联邦学习的MSE或余弦对齐机制，强制不同客户端的特征表示在嵌入空间中以逐元素方式匹配全局原型。这假设所有客户端都应将其表示映射到全局原型定义的特征子空间中，这在客户端具有相同特征提取器的同质FL中合理，但在异构FL中存在问题，因为异质特征提取器自然诱导客户端特定的特征子空间，强制所有客户端在单一全局子空间中优化不必要地抑制了其学习能力。

Method: 提出FedSAF方法，将对齐目标从绝对坐标转向类间关系结构。观察到坐标对齐隐式耦合了两个目标：对齐对分类直接有益的类间语义结构，以及强制共享特征基（在模型异质性下不必要甚至有害）。通过结构对齐而非坐标对齐来处理异构特征提取器诱导的客户端特定特征子空间。

Result: 在多个基准测试上的实验表明，结构对齐在异构设置中持续优于坐标对齐，FedSAF在原型基的异构联邦学习方法中性能提升高达3.52%。

Conclusion: 针对异构联邦学习，结构对齐比传统坐标对齐更有效，因为它解耦了类间语义对齐和共享特征基的强制，允许客户端在保持各自特征子空间的同时实现有效的知识共享，从而提升模型性能。

Abstract: Heterogeneous federated learning (HtFL) aims to enable collaboration among clients that differ in both data distributions and model architectures. Prototype-based methods, which communicate class-level feature centers (prototypes) instead of full model parameters, have recently shown strong potential for HtFL. Existing prototype-based HtFL methods typically reuse the MSE-based or cosine-based alignment mechanism developed for homogeneous FL when aligning client-specific representations with global prototypes. These approaches are essentially coordinate alignment, where representations of clients are forced to match the global prototypes in the embedding space in an element-wise manner. Such alignment implicitly assumes that all clients should map their representations into the feature subspace defined by the global prototypes. This assumption is reasonable in homogeneous FL, where all clients share the same feature extractor. However, it becomes problematic in HtFL, since heterogeneous feature extractors naturally induce client-specific feature subspaces, and forcing all clients to optimize within a single global subspace unnecessarily suppresses their learning capacity. We observe that coordinate alignment implicitly couples two distinct objectives: aligning inter-class semantic structure, which is directly beneficial for classification, and enforcing a shared feature basis, which is unnecessary and even harmful under model heterogeneity. Building on this insight, we design FedSAF, which shifts the alignment objective from absolute coordinates to inter-class relational structure. We demonstrate that structural alignment consistently outperforms coordinate alignment in heterogeneous settings. Experiments on multiple benchmarks show that our structural alignment outperforms state-of-the-art prototype-based HtFL methods by up to 3.52\%.

</details>


### [197] [BehaviorGuard: Online Backdoor Defense for Deep Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05977)
*Yinbo Yu,Xueyu Yin,Jiadai Wang,Chunwei Tian,Sai Xu,Qi Zhu,Daoqiang Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: BehaviorGuard是一种针对深度强化学习（DRL）的在线行为后门防御框架，通过检测动作分布中的行为漂移来识别和抑制后门行为，无需依赖触发器逆向工程或模型微调。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前DRL后门防御主要依赖奖励异常来逆向工程触发器并通过模型微调移除后门。但复杂触发器模式会削弱这些方法的鲁棒性，而微调成本高昂，限制了实际应用。因此，研究转向关注与触发器无关的后门输出行为。

Method: 研究发现无论攻击方式如何，后门策略都会导致动作分布出现一致性偏移以确保可靠激活，即使在没有触发器的情况下也会在高分位数区域和分布尾部留下可检测痕迹。基于此，设计了一种新颖的度量指标来捕获动作分布中的行为漂移，以在运行时识别和抑制后门动作。

Result: 在多种基准测试和不同后门攻击下的评估显示，BehaviorGuard在效果和效率上持续超越先前方法。这是首个能够同时对抗单智能体和多智能体DRL攻击的在线后门防御方法。

Conclusion: BehaviorGuard通过关注后门策略的行为特征而非触发器本身，提供了一种高效、实用的在线后门检测和缓解框架，显著提升了DRL系统的安全性。

Abstract: Backdoor attacks pose a serious threat to deep reinforcement learning (DRL). Current defenses typically rely on reward anomalies to reverse-engineer triggers and model finetuning to remove backdoors. However, complex trigger patterns undermine their robustness, and fine-tuning entails high costs, limiting practical utility. Therefore, we shift defense concerns to trigger-agnostic backdoor output behaviors and propose BehaviorGuard, an online behavior-based backdoor detection and mitigation framework for DRL. Specifically, we find that regardless of attacks, backdoored policies induce consistent shifts in action distributions to ensure reliable activation, leaving detectable traces in high-quantile regions and distribution tails, even in the absence of triggers. Based on this, we design a novel metric that captures behavioral drift in action distributions to identify and suppress backdoor actions at runtime. To our knowledge, this is the first online backdoor defense that counters attacks both in single- and multi-agent DRL. Evaluated across diverse benchmarks with different backdoor attacks, BehaviorGuard consistently surpasses prior methods in both efficacy and efficiency.

</details>


### [198] [TACT: Mitigating Overthinking and Overacting in Coding Agents via Activation Steering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05980)
*Yuan Sui,Yulin Chen,Yibo Li,Xue Jiang,Yufei He,Yihong Dong,Xiaoxin He,Tianyu Gao,Bryan Hooi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: TACT（通过激活导向的思维-行动校准）是一种检测和缓解语言模型智能体在复杂软件工程任务中长期轨迹中出现的智能体漂移的方法，通过识别和纠正"过度思考"和"过度行动"两种失败模式来提升智能体的性能和效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言模型智能体在处理复杂软件工程任务时，在长期轨迹中会出现性能退化（称为智能体漂移），主要表现为两种重复性失败模式：过度思考（反复推理已有信息）和过度行动（不整合最新观察或获取新证据就发出工具调用）。

Method: TACT方法将轨迹步骤标记为过度思考、过度行动或校准状态，发现它们在隐藏状态中可沿两个漂移轴线性分离。通过在测试时将每个步骤的激活投影到这些轴上，并将漂移的激活拉回校准区域来缓解智能体漂移。

Result: 实验表明TACT在SWE-bench Verified、Terminal-Bench 2.0和CLAW-Eval基准上优于未导向的基线，在Qwen3.5-27B上平均解决率提升+5.8个百分点，在Gemma-4-26B-A4B-it上提升+4.8个百分点，同时将解决步骤减少高达26%。

Conclusion: 智能体漂移是残差流中可导向的方向，TACT为实现可靠的长期视野智能体提供了可行的处理方法，为缓解智能体在复杂任务中的性能退化提供了有效的技术途径。

Abstract: When language model agents tackle complex software engineering tasks, they often degrade over long trajectories, which we define as *agent drift*. We focus on two recurring failure modes *overthinking* and *overacting*, i.e., where the agent repeatedly reasons over information it already has, and where it issues tool calls without integrating recent observations or acquiring new evidence. In this paper, we introduce TACT (Think-Act Calibration via activation Steering), to detect and mitigate agent drift in the residual stream before it surfaces as a behavioral failure. In specific, we label trajectory steps as overthinking, overacting, or calibrated, and find that their hidden states can separate linearly along two *drift axes*, pointing from calibrated behavior toward each failure mode (AUC $\approx$ 0.9). To mitigate agent drift, we project each step's activation onto these axes at test time and pull drifted ones back toward the calibrated region. Experiments show that TACT outperforms unsteered baselines across SWE-bench Verified, Terminal-Bench 2.0, and CLAW-Eval, lifting average resolve rate by $+5.8$ pp on Qwen3.5-27B and $+4.8$ pp on Gemma-4-26B-A4B-it while cutting steps-to-resolve by up to $26\%$. These gains frame agent drift as a steerable direction in the residual stream, and position TACT as a viable handle for reliable long-horizon agents.

</details>


### [199] [Novelty-based Tree-of-Thought Search for LLM Reasoning and Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06040)
*Leon Hamm,Zlatan Ajanovic*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 将宽度优先搜索中的新颖性概念引入语言领域，通过基于LLM的新颖性度量来修剪思维树，减少搜索空间和token成本，提高推理性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管思维链、思维树和强化学习等方法改进了LLM在推理和规划任务中的表现，但这些方法仍然脆弱，未能达到人类水平，且常伴随较高的时间和token成本。受宽度优先搜索在规划中成功的启发，研究者希望将新颖性概念迁移到语言领域来改进思维树推理。

Method: 提出了一个可测量的新颖性概念，描述新节点（思维）相对于搜索树中已有节点的独特性。通过提示LLM并利用预训练中嵌入的通用知识来估计新颖性。将此度量用于剪枝分支、缩小搜索范围。虽然每个状态需要更多提示，但通过剪枝减少总体树规模可以降低token成本。

Result: 在多个语言规划基准测试中对该方法进行了验证和比较，结果显示通过新颖性度量进行剪枝能有效控制搜索规模。

Conclusion: 将规划中的宽度搜索新颖性概念成功迁移到语言领域，基于LLM的新颖性度量可有效指导思维树剪枝，在保持或提升性能的同时降低计算成本，为改进LLM推理提供了新途径。

Abstract: Although advances such as chain-of-thought, tree-of-thought or reinforcement learning have improved the performance of LLMs in reasoning and planning tasks, they are still brittle and have not achieved human-level performance in many domains, and often suffer from high time and token costs. Inspired by the success of width-based search in planning, we explore how the concept of novelty can be transferred to language domains and how it can improve tree-of-thought reasoning. A tree of thoughts relies on building possible "paths" of consecutive ideas or thoughts. These are generated by repeatedly prompting an LLM. In our paper, a measurable concept of novelty is proposed that describes the uniqueness of a new node (thought) in comparison to nodes previously seen in the search tree. Novelty is estimated by prompting an LLM and making use of embedded general knowledge from pre-training. This metric can then be used to prune branches and reduce the scope of the search. Although this method introduces more prompts per state, the overall token cost can be reduced by pruning and reducing the overall tree size. This procedure is tested and compared using several benchmarks in language-based planning and general reasoning.

</details>


### [200] [On Time, Within Budget: Constraint-Driven Online Resource Allocation for Agentic Workflows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06110)
*Xinglin Wang,Zishen Liu,Shaoxiong Feng,Peiwen Yuan,Yiwei Li,Jiayi Shi,Yueqi Zhang,Chuyi Tan,Ji Zhang,Boyuan Pan,Yao Hu,Kan Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文研究智能体工作流在预算和截止时间约束下的在线资源分配问题，提出MCPP方法通过蒙特卡洛模拟和重规划来提高约束条件下的工作流完成概率


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实际部署中智能体系统需要满足具体的预算和截止时间约束，而非单纯优化平均效率。当前工作流执行缺乏在明确预算和截止时间约束下最大化成功完成概率的方法。

Method: 提出蒙特卡洛投资组合规划(MCPP)：将问题建模为有限时域随机在线分配问题，通过模拟工作流执行来直接估计约束完成概率，并在观察结果后重新规划。该方法为轻量级闭环规划器，能够跨同时可执行子任务分配模型和平行样本。

Result: 在CodeFlow和ProofFlow基准上的实验表明，MCPP在广泛的预算-截止时间约束范围内，相对于强基线方法持续提高了约束完成概率。

Conclusion: MCPP方法有效解决了智能体工作流在预算和截止时间约束下的在线资源分配问题，通过模拟驱动的闭环规划策略显著提升了实际部署中工作流的可靠性和成功率。

Abstract: Agentic systems increasingly solve complex user requests by executing orchestrated workflows, where subtasks are assigned to specialized models or tools and coordinated according to their dependencies. While recent work improves agent efficiency by optimizing the performance--cost--latency frontier, real deployments often impose concrete requirements: a workflow must be completed within a specified budget and before a specified deadline. This shifts the goal from average efficiency optimization to maximizing the probability that the entire workflow completes successfully under explicit budget and deadline constraints. We study \emph{constraint-driven online resource allocation for agentic workflows}. Given a dependency-structured workflow and estimates of success rates and generation lengths for each subtask--model pair, the executor allocates models and parallel samples across simultaneously executable subtasks while managing the remaining budget and time. We formulate this setting as a finite-horizon stochastic online allocation problem and propose \emph{Monte Carlo Portfolio Planning} (MCPP), a lightweight closed-loop planner that directly estimates constrained completion probability through simulated workflow executions and replans after observed outcomes. Experiments on CodeFlow and ProofFlow demonstrate that MCPP consistently improves constrained completion probability over strong baselines across a wide range of budget--deadline constraints.

</details>


### [201] [Pathways to AGI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06029)
*Gordon Fletcher,Saomai Vu Khan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文从批判性软件研究视角出发，提出了五个关键问题，分析生成式AI工具的发展路径、关键决策节点、不同基础模型轨迹的差异、替代项目状态，并基于此提出符合透明度、治理、福祉和可持续商业模式要求的AGI邻近能力发展建议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究的动机源于需要避免对AI现状必然性的假设，强调当前商业AI发展与社会政治经济环境的紧密联系。作者认为需要批判性和条件性地看待AGI问题，因为它本身在概念和定义上都存在问题，这种立场将引导讨论走向特定方向。

Method: 采用批判性软件研究视角，通过提出五个相互关联的研究问题进行分析：1) 生成式AI工具发展的关键路径；2) 作为杠杆节点的决策点和未成为主导的替代可能性；3) 三种基础模型轨迹(前沿专有模型、开源权重模型、特定领域主权模型)的路径差异；4) 关键杠杆节点分支出的替代项目状态及成功/失败原因；5) 基于分析提出符合特定要求的AGI邻近能力发展计划。

Result: 论文未在摘要中明确展示具体研究结果，但通过提出的分析框架和五个核心问题，建立了对AI发展路径、关键决策节点、模型轨迹差异和替代项目的系统性分析思路，最终旨在提出符合透明度、治理、福祉和可持续商业模式要求的技术社会发展方案。

Conclusion: 研究采用批判性视角分析AI发展，强调需要避免技术必然性假设，识别发展路径中的关键节点和替代可能性。通过分析三种基础模型轨迹和替代项目，为开发符合透明度、治理、福祉和可持续商业模式的AGI邻近能力提供了分析基础和政策建议。

Abstract: Our focus are five related questions that stem from a critical software studies perspective. Underpinning this view is the acknowledged need to avoid assumptions regarding the inevitability of the current situation relating to AI. What we need to see is the closeness of the linkage between current commercial AI development and our prevailing social, political and economic circumstances. This does mean that the perspectives presented here are done so critically and conditionally. Most importantly, Artificial General Intelligence (AGI) is seen as being problematic both conceptually and definitionally. This conditioning of any view regarding AGI does lead the discussion in specific directions and to certain conclusions regarding the future. However, adopting this perspective enables the work to offer some final recommendations. We set out to ask the following questions, 1. What are the critical pathways that produced the current dominant generative AI tools (capabilities, product forms, adoption patterns)? 2. Which decision points acted as leverage nodes (small changes that had large downstream effects), and which dead ends reveal alternative possibilities that did not become dominant? 3. How do pathways differ across three foundational-model trajectories such as the frontier proprietary models, open-weight models or specific domain and sovereign models? 4. Which alternative projects branched from key leverage nodes, what is their current state, and why did some succeed, stall, fail or become absorbed? 5. Based on this analysis, what socio-technical development programmes could plausibly move toward AGI-adjacent capability while meeting requirements for transparency, moderation, wellbeing and sustainable business models?

</details>


### [202] [Rethinking Adapter Placement: A Dominant Adaptation Module Perspective](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06183)
*Suoxin Zhang,Run He,Di Fang,Xiang Tan,Kaixuan Chen,Huiping Zhuang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出了PAGE梯度敏感性探针来识别LoRA适配器的最佳放置位置，发现了"主导适应模块"现象，并基于此提出了DomLoRA方法，仅需约0.7%的参数就能超越标准LoRA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LoRA方法虽然使用较少适配器但仍能保持或改进性能，但适配器分布广泛，对于如何放置有限数量的适配器以最大化性能仍缺乏系统性指导。研究旨在解决LoRA适配器的最佳放置位置问题。

Method: 提出PAGE（Projected Adapter Gradient Energy）梯度敏感性探针，用于估计每个候选LoRA适配器的初始可训练梯度能量。通过分析发现梯度能量高度集中在浅层FFN下投影模块（主导适应模块）。基于这一发现，提出DomLoRA方法，仅在主导适应模块放置单个适配器。

Result: PAGE探针发现在两个模型家族和四个下游任务中，梯度能量高度集中在单个浅层FFN下投影模块。该模块的层索引是架构依赖但任务稳定的。DomLoRA仅使用约0.7%的vanilla LoRA可训练参数，在指令遵循、数学推理、代码生成和多轮对话等任务上平均表现优于标准LoRA。该方法也能改进其他LoRA变体。

Conclusion: 研究发现LoRA适配器存在主导适应模块现象，该模块在模型架构中是确定且稳定的。提出的DomLoRA方法基于这一发现，为LoRA适配器放置提供了实用指南，能以极少的参数实现优于标准LoRA的性能，支持主导适应模块视角作为有效的放置策略。

Abstract: Low-rank adaptation (LoRA) is a widely used parameter-efficient fine-tuning method that places trainable low-rank adapters into frozen pre-trained models. Recent studies show that using fewer LoRA adapters may still maintain or even improve performance, but existing methods still distribute adapters broadly, leaving where to place a limited number of adapters to maximize performance largely open. To investigate this, we introduce PAGE (Projected Adapter Gradient Energy), a gradient-based sensitivity probe that estimates the initial trainable gradient energy available to each candidate LoRA adapter. Surprisingly, we find that PAGE is highly concentrated on a single shallow FFN down-projection across two model families and four downstream tasks. We term this module the dominant adaptation module and show that its layer index is architecture-dependent but task-stable. Motivated by this finding, we propose DomLoRA, a placement method that places a single adapter at the dominant adaptation module. With only ~0.7% of vanilla LoRA's trainable parameters, DomLoRA outperforms it on average across various downstream tasks, including instruction following, mathematical reasoning, code generation, and multi-turn conversation. This method also improves other LoRA variants, supporting the dominant adaptation module perspective as a practical placement guideline.

</details>


### [203] [OPSD Compresses What RLVR Teaches: A Post-RL Compaction Stage for Reasoning Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06188)
*Jaehoon Kim,Dongha Lee*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 在数学推理任务中，研究发现On-Policy Self-Distillation（OPSD）主要起压缩机制作用而非修正机制，基于此提出了SFT→RLVR→OPSD的优化后训练流程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: OPSD在具有可验证奖励的强化学习（RLVR）中被提出，声称能通过基于特权上下文的自教师进行token级信用分配，实现更高精度和更短响应。然而在启用思考的数学推理任务中，这种优势并未体现，精度增益反而可能下降。

Method: 将OPSD分别应用于正确和错误的rollout组，以独立观察压缩和修正效果。研究验证了假设：在长思考轨迹中，后见监督更容易识别冗余而非提供更好替代。

Result: 在启用思考的数学推理中，仅对正确rollout应用OPSD能保持精度同时显著缩短响应；仅对错误rollout应用则会损害精度。这表明OPSD主要作为压缩机制而非修正机制。

Conclusion: 提出了优化的后训练流程：监督微调（SFT）→基于可验证奖励的强化学习（RLVR）→策略内自蒸馏（OPSD），以在数学推理任务中实现最佳性能。

Abstract: On-Policy Self-Distillation (OPSD) has recently emerged as an alternative to Reinforcement Learning with Verifiable Rewards (RLVR), promising higher accuracy and shorter responses through token-level credit assignment from a self-teacher conditioned on privileged context. However, this promise does not carry over to thinking-enabled mathematical reasoning, where reported accuracy gains shrink and sometimes turn negative. We hypothesize that hindsight supervision can specify better token-level alternatives in short thinking-disabled outputs, but in long thinking-enabled traces it more readily identifies redundancy than supplies better replacements. To test this, we applied OPSD separately to correct and incorrect rollout groups, so that compression and correction can be observed in isolation. Our results show that in thinking-enabled mathematical reasoning, OPSD behaves most reliably as a compression mechanism rather than a correction mechanism: training only on correct rollouts preserves accuracy while substantially shortening responses, whereas training only on incorrect rollouts damages accuracy. In light of these findings, we propose a revised post-training pipeline for thinking-enabled mathematical reasoning: SFT then RLVR then OPSD.

</details>


### [204] [Visual Fingerprints for LLM Generation Comparison](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06054)
*Amal Alnouri,Andreas Hinterreiter,Christina Humer,Furui Cheng,Marc Streit*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 开发了一种通过"视觉指纹"可视化比较不同生成条件下大语言模型输出的方法，能够揭示模型行为中的一致模式


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解不同生成条件（提示、系统指令、模型参数等）如何影响LLM输出至关重要，但由于文本生成的随机性和开放性，这仍然具有挑战性。传统方法难以从分布层面比较不同条件下的模型行为倾向。

Method: 1. 将LLM响应建模为语言选择的集合（内容、表达、结构）。2. 使用自然语言处理管道提取这些选择。3. 在重复样本中表示这些选择的分布。4. 将这些分布可视化为"视觉指纹"，实现条件特定倾向的直接、分布级比较。

Result: 通过四个使用场景，展示了视觉指纹如何揭示LLM行为中难以通过单个响应或聚合指标观察的一致模式。该方法能够比较不同生成条件下的输出分布。

Conclusion: 提出的视觉指纹方法提供了一种有效工具，用于直观比较不同生成条件下LLM的输出倾向，有助于更好地理解模型行为、优化提示设计和进行模型评估。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) outputs arise from complex interactions among prompts, system instructions, model parameters, and architecture. We refer to specific configurations of these factors as generation conditions, each of which can bias outputs in various ways. Understanding how different generation conditions shape model behaviors is essential for tasks such as prompt design and model evaluation, yet it remains challenging due to the stochastic and open-ended nature of text generation. We present an approach to visually compare LLM outputs across generation conditions by modeling responses as collections of linguistic choices, including content, expression, and structure. We extract these choices using natural language processing pipelines and represent their distributions across repeated samples. We then visualize these distributions as visual fingerprints, enabling direct, distribution-level comparison of condition-specific tendencies. Through four usage scenarios, we demonstrate how visual fingerprints reveal consistent patterns in LLM behavior that are difficult to observe through individual responses or aggregate metrics.

</details>


### [205] [The Granularity Axis: A Micro-to-Macro Latent Direction for Social Roles in Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06196)
*Chonghan Qin,Xiachong Feng,Ziyun Song,Xiaocheng Feng,Jing Xiong,Lingpeng Kong*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 研究发现大语言模型内部表征确实编码了社会角色的粒度信息（从微观个体到宏观机构），并识别出粒度轴作为组织社会角色的主导几何轴，该轴具有因果可操控性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管大语言模型经常被提示扮演各种社会角色（从个体到机构），但目前尚不清楚其内部表征是否真正编码了这种从微观经验到宏观推理的角色粒度差异。

Method: 定义基于对比的粒度轴（宏观与微观角色隐藏状态均值之差），使用Qwen3-8B和Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct模型。构建5个粒度级别共75个社会角色，收集91,200个角色条件化响应。通过主成分分析、隐藏状态投影、激活引导等方法分析角色表征几何结构和因果操控性。

Result: 粒度轴与角色表征空间主成分轴高度对齐（余弦相似度0.972），解释了52.6%的方差。角色投影在5个粒度级别上单调递增，在不同层、提示变体、端点定义等条件下保持稳定。激活引导能按预测方向改变响应粒度，Llama模型在正引导下从2.00提升到3.17（五级宏观尺度）。

Conclusion: 社会角色粒度并非仅是表层风格特征，而是语言模型角色条件化行为中结构化、有序且因果可操控的潜在方向，不同模型的可操控性差异表明引导效果依赖于各模型的默认操作模式。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are routinely prompted to take on social roles ranging from individuals to institutions, yet it remains unclear whether their internal representations encode the granularity of such roles, from micro-level individual experience to macro-level organizational, institutional, or national reasoning. We show that they do. We define a contrast-based Granularity Axis as the difference between mean macro- and micro-role hidden states. In Qwen3-8B, this axis aligns with the principal axis (PC1) of the role representation space at cosine 0.972 and accounts for 52.6% of its variance, indicating that granularity is the dominant geometric axis organizing prompted social roles. We construct 75 social roles across five granularity levels and collect 91,200 role-conditioned responses over shared questions and prompt variants, then extract role-level hidden states and project them onto the axis. Role projections increase monotonically across all five levels, remain stable across layers, prompt variants, endpoint definitions, held-out splits, and score-filtered subsets, and transfer to Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct. The axis is also causally relevant: activation steering along it shifts response granularity in the predicted direction, with Llama moving from 2.00 to 3.17 on a five-point macro scale under positive steering on prompts that admit local responses. The two models differ in controllability, suggesting that steering depends on each model's default operating regime. Overall, our findings suggest that social role granularity is not merely a stylistic surface feature, but a structured, ordered, and causally manipulable latent direction in role-conditioned language model behavior.

</details>


### [206] [Patch-Effect Graph Kernels for LLM Interpretability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06480)
*Ruben Fernandez-Boullon,David N. Olivieri*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了一个将机制可解释性重新构建为图机器学习问题的框架，将激活修补配置文件表示为模型组件上的修补效应图，并评估其在GPT-2 Small上的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机制可解释性通过激活修补识别因果电路，但将这些干预扩展到不同提示和任务家族会产生高维、非结构化数据集，难以系统比较。

Method: 提出框架将机制分析重新构建为图机器学习问题，将激活修补配置文件表示为模型组件上的修补效应图。引入三种图构建方法：直接影响（因果中介）、偏相关和共同影响，并应用图核分析结果结构。

Result: 在GPT-2 Small上使用间接宾语识别（IOI）及相关任务评估，发现修补效应图保留区分性结构信号。局部边槽特征比全局图形描述符提供更高的分类准确率。验证表明共同影响和偏相关选择的候选边比随机或低秩候选具有更强的激活影响效应。

Conclusion: 该框架提供了在受控基线下比较修补派生结构的压缩和评估流程，将稳健的切片区分性证据与更强的任务通用因果电路主张分开。图特征压缩结构化修补信号，而原始张量和表面线索定义了任何电路级主张都应解决的强基线。

Abstract: Mechanistic interpretability aims to reverse-engineer transformer computations by identifying causal circuits through activation patching. However, scaling these interventions across diverse prompts and task families produces high-dimensional, unstructured datasets that are difficult to compare systematically. We propose a framework that reframes mechanistic analysis as a graph machine-learning problem by representing activation-patching profiles as patch-effect graphs over model components. We introduce three graph-construction methods: direct-influence via causal mediation, partial-correlation, and co-influence and apply graph kernels to analyze the resulting structures. Evaluating this approach on GPT-2 Small using Indirect Object Identification (IOI) and related tasks, we find that patch-effect graphs preserve discriminative structural signals. Specifically, localized edge-slot features provide higher classification accuracy than global graph-shape descriptors. A screened paired-patching validation suggests that CI and PC selected candidate edges correspond to stronger activation-influence effects than random or low-rank candidates. Crucially, by evaluating these representations against rigorous prompt-only and raw patch-effect controls, we make the evidential scope of the benchmark explicit: graph features compress structured patching signal, while raw tensors and surface cues define strong baselines that any circuit-level claim should address. Ultimately, our framework provides a compression and evaluation pipeline for comparing patching-derived structures under controlled baselines, separating robust slice-discriminative evidence from stronger task-general causal-circuit claims.

</details>


### [207] [Safety Certification is Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06087)
*Oliver Schön,Licio Romao,Sadegh Soudjani*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于核嵌入框架的安全认证方法，将安全验证转化为轨迹数据上的分类问题，直接估计T步安全概率而无需递归计算，避免了传统动态规划方法中的累积误差问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有安全认证方法使用轨迹数据估计转移概率，并通过动态规划递归计算安全概率。这种递归会导致累积误差，随着时间步长T增长，认证的安全概率下限会退化到无意义的水平。

Method: 提出核嵌入框架，将安全认证视为轨迹数据上的分类问题。该框架直接估计T步安全概率，避免递归计算。该框架可以包含文献中的已知方法（如障碍证书、鲁棒马尔可夫模型）作为特例，并能超越这些方法的限制。

Result: 直接估计器在安全认证时间步长T上保持稳定，且适用于非马尔可夫动力学系统。而基于动态规划的认证方法会静默失效。在神经控制四旋翼无人机上的仿真验证了这一结果。

Conclusion: 所提出的核嵌入框架避免了安全认证中的累积误差问题，支持非马尔可夫系统，并且在认证时间步长T增加时保持稳定，比传统的动态规划方法更具鲁棒性。

Abstract: The goal of this paper is certifying safety of dynamical systems subject to uncertainty. Existing approaches use trajectory data to estimate transition probabilities, and compute safety probabilities recursively via dynamic programming (DP). This recursion may lead to compounding errors in the certified safety probability, thus collapsing to a vacuous lower bound for growing horizons $T$. We propose a kernel embedding framework that treats safety certification as a classification problem on trajectory data, directly estimating the $T$-step safety probability without recursion. We show that the framework subsumes well-established approaches from the literature (e.g., barrier certificates, robust Markov models) as special cases, and allows us to go beyond their limitations. As the main consequence, it bypasses compounding error across the horizon and enables certification for systems with non-Markovian dynamics. We demonstrate that direct estimators remain stable independent of the certification horizon and in the non-Markovian setting, whilst DP-based certificates silently go unsound -- confirmed in simulation on a neural-controlled quadrotor.

</details>


### [208] [Shallow Prefill, Deep Decoding: Efficient Long-Context Inference via Layer-Asymmetric KV Visibility](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06105)
*Jungsuk Oh,Hyeseo Jeon,Hyunjune Ji,Kyongmin Kong,Jay-Yoon Lee*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SPEED是一种针对仅解码器语言模型的长上下文推理优化方法，通过浅层预填充+深度解码的相位不对称KV可见性策略，在保留基准测试质量的同时显著降低计算和内存成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前仅解码器语言模型在长上下文推理时成本高昂，因为长提示需要在预填充阶段在所有层处理并缓存，并在解码阶段重复关注，导致计算开销大和内存占用高。

Method: 提出SPEED（浅层预填充、深度解码）方法，采用相位不对称的KV可见性策略：只在下层对非锚点提示token的KV状态进行具体化，而解码阶段token保持全深度。仅保留最小BOS锚点，将预填充token从上层解码可见性集中完全移除。

Result: 在Llama-3.1-8B指令调优研究中，SPEED使用75%的层处理预填充token，在OLMES风格基准测试中获得51.2平均分（全深度基线为51.4），同时提高TTFT 33%、TPOT 22%，在128K上下文下减少活动KV内存25.0%。

Conclusion: 长上下文提示token不需要始终作为全深度KV缓存对象存在，当解码阶段token保持全深度时，SPEED的浅层预填充方法能在保持模型质量的同时显著降低计算和内存开销。

Abstract: Long-context inference in decoder-only language models is costly because long prompts are processed during Prefill, cached at every layer, and repeatedly attended to during autoregressive Decode. We introduce \emph{Shallow Prefill, dEEp Decode} (SPEED), a phase-asymmetric KV-visibility policy that materializes non-anchor prompt-token KV states only in lower layers while keeping Decode-phase tokens full-depth. Unlike previous approaches that make upper-layer prompt KV states cheaper to store or construct, SPEED removes prefill tokens from the upper-layer Decode visibility set altogether. With a minimal BoS anchor, this simple change preserves broad benchmark quality while reducing long-context cost. In a controlled Llama-3.1-8B instruction-tuning study, SPEED using only 75\% of layers for prefill tokens reaches 51.2 average score on OLMES-style benchmarks, compared with 51.4 for the full-depth baseline, while improving TTFT by 33\%, TPOT by 22\%, and reducing active KV memory by 25.0\% at 128K context. Layer-wise diagnostics suggest that this cutoff retains the main prompt-selection and representation-stabilization regions of the full-depth model. These results show that long-context prompt tokens need not always persist as full-depth KV-cache objects when Decode-phase tokens remain full-depth.

</details>


### [209] [MASPO: Joint Prompt Optimization for LLM-based Multi-Agent Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06623)
*Zhexuan Wang,Xuebo Liu,Li Wang,Zifei Shan,Yutong Wang,Zhenxi Song,Min Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: MASPO：一个自动迭代优化多智能体系统中角色特定提示的框架，通过联合评估机制和数据驱动的进化束搜索，在不依赖真实标签的情况下提升系统整体性能


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于LLM的多智能体系统在复杂协作任务中展现出潜力，但提示质量对性能至关重要。现有方法面临挑战：难以联合优化多个交互智能体的提示，主要因为本地智能体目标与整体系统目标之间的不对齐问题。

Method: MASPO框架包含两个核心创新：1) 联合评估机制 - 评估提示不仅基于本地有效性，更看重其促进下游后继智能体成功的能力，从而桥接本地交互与全局结果；2) 数据驱动的进化束搜索 - 高效探索高维提示空间。

Result: 在6个多样化任务上的广泛实证评估表明，MASPO始终优于最先进的提示优化方法，平均准确率提升2.9%。

Conclusion: MASPO为解决多智能体系统中提示优化问题提供了有效框架，通过联合评估机制和数据驱动的搜索策略，能够自动优化系统级性能而无需真实标签，在多个任务上展现出卓越性能。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM)-based Multi-agent systems (MAS) have shown promise in tackling complex collaborative tasks, where agents are typically orchestrated via role-specific prompts. While the quality of these prompts is pivotal, jointly optimizing them across interacting agents remains a non-trivial challenge, primarily due to the misalignment between local agent objectives and holistic system goals. To address this, we introduce MASPO, a novel framework designed to automatically and iteratively refine prompts across the entire system. A core innovation of MASPO is its joint evaluation mechanism, which assesses prompts not merely by their local validity, but by their capacity to facilitate downstream success for successor agents. This effectively bridges the gap between local interactions and global outcomes without relying on ground-truth labels. Furthermore, MASPO employs a data-driven evolutionary beam search to efficiently navigate the high-dimensional prompt space. Extensive empirical evaluations across 6 diverse tasks demonstrate that MASPO consistently outperforms state-of-the-art prompt optimization methods, achieving an average accuracy improvement of 2.9. We release our code at https://github.com/wangzx1219/MASPO.

</details>


### [210] [Can RL Teach Long-Horizon Reasoning to LLMs? Expressiveness Is Key](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06638)
*Tianle Wang,Zhaoyang Wang,Guangchen Lan,Xinpeng Wei,Sipeng Zhang,Guanwen Qiu,Abulhair Saparov*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: ScaleLogic：一个控制推理难度（深度和逻辑表达能力）的合成框架，揭示RL训练计算量与推理深度呈幂律关系，且更复杂的逻辑训练能带来更好的下游任务性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管强化学习已被应用于改进大语言模型的推理能力，但训练规模如何随任务难度扩展的系统性研究一直受限于缺乏可控、可扩展的环境。目前缺少能够独立控制推理难度维度的框架。

Method: 提出了ScaleLogic框架，这是一个合成逻辑推理框架，可以独立控制两个难度维度：1）所需证明规划的深度（即推理步数），2）底层逻辑的表达能力。框架支持从简单蕴含逻辑到包含合取、析取、否定和全称量词的一阶推理等多种逻辑。使用该框架研究RL训练计算量与推理深度之间的关系。

Result: RL训练计算量T与推理深度D呈幂律关系（T ∝ D^γ，R² > 0.99），缩放指数γ随逻辑表达能力单调增加（从1.04到2.60）。在下游数学和一般推理基准测试中，更具表达能力的训练设置带来更大的性能提升（高达+10.66分）和更高效的计算迁移。幂律关系在多种RL方法中均成立，基于课程的学习显著提高了缩放效率。

Conclusion: ScaleLogic框架为系统研究RL训练扩展性提供了可控环境，证明了训练内容（不仅仅是训练量）对下游迁移效果有重要影响。更具表达能力的逻辑训练能带来更好的性能和计算效率，幂律关系为理解RL训练扩展性提供了理论基础。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) has been applied to improve large language model (LLM) reasoning, yet the systematic study of how training scales with task difficulty has been hampered by the lack of controlled, scalable environments. We introduce ScaleLogic, a synthetic logical reasoning framework that offers independent control over two axes of difficulty: the depth of the required proof planning (i.e., the horizon) and the expressiveness of the underlying logic. Our proposed framework supports a wide range of logics: from simple implication-only logic ("if-then") towards more expressive first-order reasoning with conjunction ("and"), disjunction ("or"), negation ("not"), and universal quantification ("for all"). Using this framework, we show that the RL training compute $T$ follows a power law with respect to reasoning depth $D$ ($T \propto D^γ$, $R^{2} > 0.99$), and that the scaling exponent $γ$ increases monotonically with logical expressiveness, from $1.04$ to $2.60$. On downstream mathematics and general reasoning benchmarks, more expressive training settings yield both larger performance gains (up to $+10.66$ points) and more compute-efficient transfer compared to less expressive settings, demonstrating that what a model is trained on, not just how much it is trained, shapes downstream transfer. We further show that the power-law relationship holds across multiple RL methods, and curriculum-based training substantially improves scaling efficiency.

</details>


### [211] [CrossCult-KIBench: A Benchmark for Cross-Cultural Knowledge Insertion in MLLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06115)
*Zhen Zeng,Leijiang Gu,Feng Li,Jing Yu,Zenglin Shi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出跨文化知识插入任务，介绍CrossCult-KIBench评估基准和MCKI基线方法，用于评估MLLMs在文化适配与行为保留方面的平衡


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态大语言模型主要基于英语数据训练，在跨文化场景中经常生成文化不适当或不一致的响应，需要解决文化适应性问题

Method: 提出跨文化知识插入任务，创建包含9,800个图像基础案例的CrossCult-KIBench评估基准，涵盖英语、中文和阿拉伯语文化群体；提出基于外部记忆检索的MCKI方法作为基线

Result: 实验显示当前方法在文化适应性和行为保留之间难以取得平衡，揭示了开发文化感知MLLMs的关键挑战

Conclusion: 这项工作强调了开发更具文化适应性和负责任MLLMs的重要研究方向，为评估文化知识插入的有效性和副作用提供了系统性框架

Abstract: Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs), trained primarily on English-centric data, frequently generate culturally inappropriate or misaligned responses in cross-cultural settings. To mitigate this, we introduce the task of cross-cultural knowledge insertion, which focuses on adapting models to specific cultural contexts while preserving their original behavior in other cultures. To facilitate research in this area, we introduce CrossCult-KIBench, a comprehensive evaluation benchmark for assessing both the effectiveness of knowledge insertion and its unintended side effects on non-target cultures. The benchmark includes 9,800 image-grounded cases covering 49 culturally relevant visual scenarios across English, Chinese, and Arabic language-culture groups. It supports evaluation in both single-insert and sequential-insert settings. We also propose Memory-Conditioned Knowledge Insertion (MCKI) as a baseline method. MCKI retrieves relevant cultural knowledge from an external memory using frozen MLLM representations, prepending matched entries as conditional prompts when applicable. Extensive experiments on CrossCult-KIBench reveal that current approaches struggle to balance effective cultural adaptation with behavioral preservation, highlighting a key challenge in developing culturally-aware MLLMs. Our work thus underscores an important research direction for developing more culturally adaptive and responsible MLLMs.

</details>


### [212] [Back to the Beginning of Heuristic Design: Bridging Code and Knowledge with LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06123)
*Nguyen Viet Tuan Kiet,Bui Dinh Pham,Dao Van Tung,Tran Cong Dao,Huynh Thi Thanh Binh*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新的自动启发式设计范式：从传统的自底向上代码搜索转向自顶向下知识优先搜索，将知识作为主要搜索对象，代码仅作为实例化和测试工具，提高了发现效率、可迁移性和泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的大语言模型自动启发式设计方法主要采用自底向上范式，通过执行候选程序并从执行反馈中提取见解来迭代改进。这种从低层实现到高层原则的方法存在局限性。作者认为这种视角不完整，需要引入互补的自顶向下视角。

Method: 通过统计学习视角形式化这一转变，揭示失真-压缩权衡。在两种自动启发式设计框架（基于种群的和基于树的）中实例化这一理念。将知识作为主要搜索对象，代码仅用于实例化和测试知识，使所学内容显式化并可在不同问题和搜索轨迹中重用。

Result: 在组合优化和其他任务中，知识优先搜索相比代码中心方法提高了发现效率、可迁移性和泛化能力。两种策略结合时能获得进一步增益。知识优先方法通常优于代码中心流程。

Conclusion: 自动启发式设计的进展取决于迭代构建和演化可解释的假设，这些假设的价值能够超越单个搜索轨迹。自顶向下知识优先范式与自底向上代码中心范式相辅相成，共同推动领域发展。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have recently advanced automatic heuristic design (AHD) for combinatorial optimization (CO), where candidate heuristics are iteratively proposed, evaluated, and refined. Most existing approaches search over executable programs and distill insights from execution feedback to guide later iterations. Because this process moves from low-level implementations to high-level principles, we refer to it as a bottom-up paradigm. We argue that this view is incomplete and introduce a complementary top-down perspective: knowledge becomes the primary search object and code merely instantiates and tests it, making what is learned explicit and reusable across problems and trajectories. We formalize this shift through a statistical-learning view that exposes a distortion--compression trade-off, and instantiate it in both population-based and tree-based AHD frameworks. Across CO and tasks beyond it, knowledge-first search improves discovery efficiency, transfer, and generalization, often outperforming code-centric pipelines, while combining both strategies yields further gains. Our results suggest that progress in AHD depends on iteratively constructing and evolving interpretable hypotheses that retain value beyond a single search trajectory.

</details>


### [213] [P-Guide: Parameter-Efficient Prior Steering for Single-Pass CFG Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06124)
*Xin Peng,Ang Gao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: P-Guide：通过单次前向推理实现条件流匹配的无分类器引导，减少50%推理延迟，同时保持生成质量


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统无分类器引导（CFG）在流匹配中需要每个采样步骤进行双重前向传播，导致显著的计算开销和推理延迟，这成为高效条件生成的主要瓶颈

Method: 提出P-Guide框架，通过仅调制初始潜在状态实现高质量引导。采用一阶近似证明P-Guide在从先验空间引导生成方面与CFG等效，而不需要在采样期间进行显式的速度场外推。同时考虑同方差和异方差先验，联合建模均值和方差以实现自适应损失衰减和对数据不确定性的鲁棒性

Result: 实验表明P-Guide将推理延迟减少约50%，同时在保真度和提示对齐方面保持与标准双重传播CFG基线竞争力相当的生成质量

Conclusion: P-Guide提供了一种计算高效的替代方案，能够在保持条件生成质量的同时显著减少推理开销，为实际应用中的高效流匹配生成打开了新的可能性

Abstract: Classifier-Free Guidance (CFG) is essential for high-fidelity conditional generation in flow matching, yet it imposes significant computational overhead by requiring dual forward passes at each sampling step. In this work, we address this bottleneck by introducing \textbf{P-Guide}, a framework that achieves high-quality guidance through a single inference pass by modulating only the initial latent state. We further show that, under a first-order approximation, P-Guide is equivalent to CFG in the sense that it steers generation from the prior space, without requiring explicit velocity field extrapolation during sampling. We consider both homoscedastic and \textbf{heteroscedastic} priors, and find that jointly modeling the mean and variance enables adaptive loss attenuation and improved robustness to data uncertainty. Extensive experiments demonstrate that P-Guide reduces inference latency by approximately 50\% while maintaining fidelity and prompt alignment competitive with standard dual-pass CFG baselines.

</details>


### [214] [Skill1: Unified Evolution of Skill-Augmented Agents via Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06130)
*Yaorui Shi,Yuxin Chen,Zhengxi Lu,Yuchun Miao,Shugui Liu,Qi GU,Xunliang Cai,Xiang Wang,An Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Skill1是一个统一框架，通过单一策略协同演化技能选择、使用和提炼能力，使用单任务结果信号进行训练，在ALFWorld和WebShop任务上优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有持久技能库方法孤立优化技能选择、使用和提炼能力，或使用分离的奖励信号，导致部分和冲突的演化。需要统一的框架来协同优化这些能力。

Method: Skill1训练单一策略来协同演化技能选择、使用和提炼。策略生成查询搜索技能库，重排名候选项，基于选定技能解决任务，并从轨迹中提炼新技能。所有学习都来自单一任务结果信号：低频趋势归因于技能选择，高频变化归因于技能提炼。

Result: 在ALFWorld和WebShop实验中，Skill1优于先前的基于技能的方法和强化学习基线。训练动态确认了三种能力的协同演化，消融实验显示移除任何归因信号都会降低演化效果。

Conclusion: Skill1框架通过单一任务结果信号实现了技能选择、使用和提炼的协同演化，提供了更有效的技能库维护方法，解决了现有方法中的部分和冲突演化问题。

Abstract: A persistent skill library allows language model agents to reuse successful strategies across tasks. Maintaining such a library requires three coupled capabilities. The agent selects a relevant skill, utilizes it during execution, and distills new skills from experience. Existing methods optimize these capabilities in isolation or with separate reward sources, resulting in partial and conflicting evolution.
